Confirm order · Stages 7–8OM confirms order, fulfillment and invoice details. Deployment Case · Stages 6–8Start at won decision; deployment + value continue on the Case after Stage 8. GTM Process

Stage specification

Opportunity creation

Capture source attribution and the originating Contact; record SAL on opportunity creation, or SAL and SQL for seller-qualified direct entry to Stage 1.

Owner · Sales · Marketing · Partners · BDRs · Forecast: Pre-pipeline

Required fields

Field / typeInputsCurrent purposeChangeUpdated purpose / placement

Input format / limits

Maximum characters: 120

Identify the opportunity. Identify the deal clearly.UnchangedAt creationRetain the documented purpose in Creation.

Opportunity SourcePicklist (source inventory; control not verified)
Dropdown inputs · 8 choices

Single-select

Available choices:

  • --None--
  • Direct
  • Channel Partners
  • Server Vendors
  • BDR
  • Cloud
  • GSI
  • Marketing
Identifies the opportunity source for routing and attribution; preserve the original intake and qualification path separately from current ownership.Validation / automationAt creationRequire Opportunity Source plus its applicable sourcing lookup at creation: Direct or BDR → Sourcing User; Marketing → Sourcing Campaign; every other nonblank source → Sourcing Partner. Show and require only the applicable lookup. A blank/--None-- source is not a partner selection and cannot pass validation. Preserve origin independently of assignment and Route to Market.
Sourcing UserLookup/search control (UI captured; User target to verify)

Select a related User record using the existing search control; no fixed choice list. The observed 255-character control limit is a search-input limit, not an attribution text field.

Records the person credited with sourcing the opportunity.Validation / automationMoved from Opportunity sourcing information.At creation · Direct / BDRRequired for Direct or BDR. Default Direct to the creating User; populate BDR from the originating BDR User. Reuse the existing field; preserve its label and history.
Sourcing CampaignLookup/search control (UI captured; Campaign target to verify)

Select a related Campaign record using the existing search control; no fixed choice list. The observed 255-character control limit is a search-input limit, not an attribution text field.

Records the campaign credited with sourcing the opportunity.Validation / automationMoved from Opportunity sourcing information.At creation · MarketingRequired for Marketing. Populate from the originating Campaign. Reuse the existing field; preserve its label and history. Future dependency — Marketing MAP program: today Sourcing Campaign remains the existing Pardot campaign lookup (current system described by Marketing). At the MAP cutover it will become Marketing Source, mapped from the marketing automation platform source. This is a future field/integration change, not a rename or replacement in the current release. Confirm source IDs, data model, historical campaign crosswalk and multi-touch reporting with Marketing before migration; preserve current attribution and historical campaign references. Opportunity Source still provides the high-level source classification.
Sourcing PartnerLookup/search control (UI captured; Account / partner target to verify)

Select a related Account / partner record using the existing search control; no fixed choice list. The observed 255-character control limit is a search-input limit, not an attribution text field.

Records the partner account credited with sourcing the opportunity.Validation / automationMoved from Opportunity sourcing information.At creation · Other nonblank sourcesRequired for all other nonblank Opportunity Source selections. Populate from the registering Partner Account for deal registration, or the originating partner on other partner-source paths. Reuse the existing field; preserve its label and history.
Opportunity OwnerLookup / person (source inventory; control not verified)
Current input information

No editable control captured for this display field; read-only/calculated or mapping requires confirmation.

Name the seller accountable for the opportunity.UnchangedAt creationRetain the documented purpose in Creation.
Conversion ContactRelated records

Select/link an existing Salesforce Contact; no new buying-role choice is required. Exact originating-contact relationship mapping must be confirmed.

Associates existing Contacts with the opportunity and records their buying roles.Extend trackingAt creation · originating Contact associationCapture a resolved originating Contact at creation for conversion tracking; maintain buying roles through discovery and require at least one assigned Opportunity Contact Role before Stage 2. These are separate checks. Reuse a verified existing originating/primary Contact relationship or a designated Contact Role; confirm the exact relationship and creation transaction before configuration. Primary Contact remains optional as a committee designation unless it is the approved conversion-link mapping. Do not require every buying-committee member at creation. Use an explicitly designated originating/conversion Contact linked through Opportunity Contact Roles; reuse a supported existing primary/originating Contact relationship before adding storage. Require a resolved Contact association for creation/conversion tracking and before Stage 1; do not update every buying-committee member or infer the person from an account alone. The account is the linked Contact account in the same deal context; flag conflicting associations for review. Extend the existing Contact lifecycle and Account Lifecycle Stage (ALS) mappings rather than creating parallel Contact/Account lifecycle fields. Show per-contact conversion and related-opportunity evidence on the Account; preserve its current lifecycle semantics and customer states until the rollup mapping is approved. One new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle field shows SAL/SQL for this opportunity; retain it as the last conversion milestone after discovery, separate from sales Stage.
Opportunity Conversion LifecyclePicklist · system-maintained · proposed
Proposed inputs

Proposed field; no current input control. Use the proposed type shown above.

Proposed inputs
  • SAL
  • SQL
No existing field confirmed; proposed capture.New fieldSystem-maintained · SAL at creation / SQL at Stage 1New Opportunity-level conversion milestone: SAL on creation; SQL on entry to Stage 1 or seller-direct Stage 1 creation. Separate from sales Stage and existing Contact/Account lifecycle. Retain source, qualification channel (MAP / PAX.AI / seller / partner / BDR), event timestamp, actor/system, Contact, Account and Opportunity relationship in conversion history. Reuse suitable existing lifecycle history/event storage, extending only missing data. Record MQL or AQL at Contact qualification; SAL at opportunity creation; accepted/rejected decision separately; SQL on first Stage 1 entry. Preserve every episode for multiple opportunities or requalification and deduplicate retries with the source event/episode ID. Late MQL/AQL messages, stage regression and a new Stage 0 opportunity must not downgrade a Contact or Account already further through its lifecycle. Keep opportunity-level events so separate deals remain reportable. Report MQL→SAL→SQL and AQL→SAL→SQL as separate cohorts, including rejected/pending SALs and elapsed time. Seller-direct SQLs remain a separate cohort; never inflate inbound conversion by fabricating MQL/AQL history.

Reuse the existing field; add the Pre-pipeline choice. System-maintained, not a separate seller input. Creation / Stage 0 → Pre-pipeline; Stages 1–2 → Pipeline; Stages 3–4 → Upside; Stages 5–6 → Commit; Stages 7–8 → Won; Closed lost → Loss.

Ongoing activities

  • Conversion tracking
  • Partner contribution

Automations and validations

  • Require Opportunity Source plus its applicable sourcing lookup at creation: Direct or BDR → Sourcing User; Marketing → Sourcing Campaign; every other nonblank source → Sourcing Partner. Show and require only the applicable lookup. A blank/--None-- source is not a partner selection and cannot pass validation.
  • Populate attribution before creation validation: Direct → the User creating the opportunity; BDR → the originating BDR User; Marketing → the originating Campaign; deal registration → the registering Partner Account. Other partner-source paths copy the originating Partner Account when available. For deal registration, use the mapped partner-related Opportunity Source, not Direct or BDR. For BDR and Marketing origins set the matching Opportunity Source. Use origin-record references, not a background integration user, current owner or fulfillment partner, to identify the sourcing BDR, Campaign or Partner.
  • Apply the same dependency and population rules to manual creation, conversion, imports and integrations. If the originating reference is missing or ambiguous, require the applicable selection or resolve the intake error before creating the opportunity; never invent an attribution. Do not overwrite a valid sourcing reference on routine edits or ownership changes. On an authorized source correction, require the newly applicable lookup, exclude inactive sourcing lookups from current attribution and preserve previous values and the change in audit history. Keep Opportunity Source attribution separate from Stage 1 Route to Market and from partner-contribution timing.
  • Require the core creation fields and preserve Opportunity Source when ownership changes.
  • Route unassigned opportunities to the appropriate review queue; notify the assigned seller.
  • Disable quote creation and revision. Default the creation flow to Pre-pipeline; derive the saved sales forecast from its initial stage.
  • Snapshot creation owner/channel origin for attribution; later ownership changes do not alter partner-only origin.
  • MAP supplies MQL (Marketing Qualified Lead) to the existing Contact lifecycle. PAX.AI supplies AQL (Agentic Qualified Lead) to the Contact. These are distinct entry paths, not mandatory sequential stages. Creating an opportunity records SAL (Sales Accepted Lead) for its linked conversion Contact and the new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle. Inbound marketing, BDR, partner/deal-registration and other non-self-qualified intake enters Stage 0 for SAL review. Entry to Stage 1 records SQL (Sales Qualified Lead) after acceptance and the source-dependent CHAMPS gate. A seller creating a self-qualified opportunity directly in Stage 1 may auto-accept and qualify it: record SAL and SQL events at creation, with the seller and direct-entry path; do not manufacture prior MQL/AQL events. Sales-generated CHAMPS remains required before Stage 2.
  • Use an explicitly designated originating/conversion Contact linked through Opportunity Contact Roles; reuse a supported existing primary/originating Contact relationship before adding storage. Require a resolved Contact association for creation/conversion tracking and before Stage 1; do not update every buying-committee member or infer the person from an account alone. The account is the linked Contact account in the same deal context; flag conflicting associations for review. Extend the existing Contact lifecycle and Account Lifecycle Stage (ALS) mappings rather than creating parallel Contact/Account lifecycle fields. Show per-contact conversion and related-opportunity evidence on the Account; preserve its current lifecycle semantics and customer states until the rollup mapping is approved. One new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle field shows SAL/SQL for this opportunity; retain it as the last conversion milestone after discovery, separate from sales Stage.
  • PAX.AI is the qualification channel, not a newly approved Opportunity Source choice. Its integration must provide the underlying source and applicable sourcing lookup under the current source-attribution rules; resolve that mapping before automated opportunity creation. Do not invent a campaign, sourcing user or partner, and do not classify an AI-origin record as seller-self-qualified simply because an integration created it.
  • Future dependency — Marketing MAP program: today Sourcing Campaign remains the existing Pardot campaign lookup (current system described by Marketing). At the MAP cutover it will become Marketing Source, mapped from the marketing automation platform source. This is a future field/integration change, not a rename or replacement in the current release. Confirm source IDs, data model, historical campaign crosswalk and multi-touch reporting with Marketing before migration; preserve current attribution and historical campaign references. Opportunity Source still provides the high-level source classification.
  • For forward moves through numbered sales stages, enforce all prerequisite stage gates crossed, including assignment/acceptance, discovery, technical fit/POC, Technical Win, procurement and OM booking authority. Apply the same checks to UI, imports, integrations and automation; a stage skip cannot bypass a gate. New sales opportunities start at 0, or at 1 only for the documented assigned seller-self-qualified path. Loss uses its own loss-entry rules and does not require completing earlier sales gates. Preserve the documented Stage 6 → 5 quote-correction path and booked-record locks.
  • Retain the existing Duplicate, Transacted-Internal Order and Transacted-Reference Opty administrative outcomes and their current handling. They are not additional sales stages or Closed lost outcomes. No retirement or historical migration of those values is requested by this design.
  • Implementation prerequisite — RevOps must confirm the existing Bypass Validations permissions, affected rules and audit evidence. Do not grant new bypass access or treat that field as blanket authority to ignore the new gates. Preserve approved migration/historical-record exceptions; record authorized use. Resolve the rule-by-rule scope before enabling the new validations.
  • Capture a resolved originating Contact at creation for conversion tracking; maintain buying roles through discovery and require at least one assigned Opportunity Contact Role before Stage 2. These are separate checks. Reuse a verified existing originating/primary Contact relationship or a designated Contact Role; confirm the exact relationship and creation transaction before configuration. Primary Contact remains optional as a committee designation unless it is the approved conversion-link mapping. Do not require every buying-committee member at creation.
  • Manage Forecast Category (Sales) automatically from the opportunity sales stage on creation and each permitted stage change, including UI, import and integration paths. Creation / Stage 0 → Pre-pipeline; Stages 1–2 → Pipeline; Stages 3–4 → Upside; Stages 5–6 → Commit; Stages 7–8 → Won; Closed lost → Loss. Creation is a capture step, not another Stage value: default the creation flow to Pre-pipeline, then derive the saved category from the actual initial stage. Seller-self-qualified creation directly in Stage 1 saves Pipeline immediately. An allowed return to Stage 0 restores Pre-pipeline; retain booked-record locks and existing administrative-outcome handling. Prevent or correct manual/integration category values that conflict with the stage; users do not maintain this field separately.

Validate

Assign the seller, review the SAL and qualify inbound marketing, PAX, BDR and partner/deal-registration handoffs before SQL.

SAL StatusExisting read-only display · schema/control type unverified

Existing field confirmed in Salesforce; no editable input or choice list exposed on the inspected form. Confirm its API/type and existing automation before changing the control.

Displays the existing SAL handoff disposition.Validation / automationMoved from Demand Gen / optional SAL context.Stage 0 · disposition before Stage 1Creating the opportunity records the SAL lifecycle milestone, not the AE acceptance decision. Use existing SAL Status for Pending review, Accepted and Rejected business meanings, mapped to its actual current choices before configuration. SAL AE identifies the accountable seller. For inbound records, prevent entry to Stage 1 while pending or rejected; acceptance alone does not bypass CHAMPS or assignment. For Marketing-source SALs, require an acceptance date when accepted; require rejection date and explanatory notes when rejected. Notes remain available throughout review. Stamp actor and decision time through the acceptance/rejection action rather than manual date entry. Retain submissions, start and completion history separately: do not equate SAL Start Date with acceptance or SAL Completion Date with rejection without verifying their definitions. A rejected SAL does not become SQL or automatically become a Closed lost opportunity; retain its rejection disposition for conversion reporting.

Automations and validations

  • Route by the agreed assignment rules and surface unassigned records for review.
  • All non-self-qualified inbound intake—including Marketing, partner/deal registration, BDR-origin and PAX.AI—must complete all five existing CHAMPS fields before Stage 1. Determine this from preserved intake origin and qualification path; reassignment to an AE does not remove the gate. Seller-self-qualified opportunities may start in Stage 1 and must complete CHAMPS before Stage 2. Keep CHAMPS - Challenge separate from Identify Pain.
  • Preserve original source and attribution evidence. Keep quoting disabled and Forecast Category (Sales) at Pre-pipeline.
  • Record the assigned AE acceptance decision with actor and time separately from SAL creation and SQL qualification. RevOps acting for the AE must retain evidence of that acceptance. The later 0 → 1 handoff checks acceptance, assignment and CHAMPS before recording SQL.
  • Carry forward the validated Opportunity Source and applicable Sourcing User, Campaign or Partner from creation; assignment changes must not overwrite origin attribution.
  • Creating the opportunity records the SAL lifecycle milestone, not the AE acceptance decision. Use existing SAL Status for Pending review, Accepted and Rejected business meanings, mapped to its actual current choices before configuration. SAL AE identifies the accountable seller. For inbound records, prevent entry to Stage 1 while pending or rejected; acceptance alone does not bypass CHAMPS or assignment. For Marketing-source SALs, require an acceptance date when accepted; require rejection date and explanatory notes when rejected. Notes remain available throughout review. Stamp actor and decision time through the acceptance/rejection action rather than manual date entry. Retain submissions, start and completion history separately: do not equate SAL Start Date with acceptance or SAL Completion Date with rejection without verifying their definitions. A rejected SAL does not become SQL or automatically become a Closed lost opportunity; retain its rejection disposition for conversion reporting.
  • On Stage 0 → 1, after acceptance, assignment and CHAMPS pass, stamp SQL for this Opportunity and linked Contact episode; sync the approved Account view. Retain source, qualification channel (MAP / PAX.AI / seller / partner / BDR), event timestamp, actor/system, Contact, Account and Opportunity relationship in conversion history. Reuse suitable existing lifecycle history/event storage, extending only missing data. Record MQL or AQL at Contact qualification; SAL at opportunity creation; accepted/rejected decision separately; SQL on first Stage 1 entry. Preserve every episode for multiple opportunities or requalification and deduplicate retries with the source event/episode ID. Late MQL/AQL messages, stage regression and a new Stage 0 opportunity must not downgrade a Contact or Account already further through its lifecycle. Keep opportunity-level events so separate deals remain reportable. Report MQL→SAL→SQL and AQL→SAL→SQL as separate cohorts, including rejected/pending SALs and elapsed time. Seller-direct SQLs remain a separate cohort; never inflate inbound conversion by fabricating MQL/AQL history.
  • Block Stage 1 entry until the assigned AE has accepted and the intake-path CHAMPS gate is satisfied; record SQL only at the handoff.
  • For forward moves through numbered sales stages, enforce all prerequisite stage gates crossed, including assignment/acceptance, discovery, technical fit/POC, Technical Win, procurement and OM booking authority. Apply the same checks to UI, imports, integrations and automation; a stage skip cannot bypass a gate. New sales opportunities start at 0, or at 1 only for the documented assigned seller-self-qualified path. Loss uses its own loss-entry rules and does not require completing earlier sales gates. Preserve the documented Stage 6 → 5 quote-correction path and booked-record locks.
  • Automatically maintain Forecast Category (Sales) = Pre-pipeline on entry and each permitted stage change. Apply through UI, imports and integrations; prevent or correct a value that conflicts with the stage. Keep this separate from standard Salesforce forecast fields.

On that association activation and on stage advancement, require the existing Opportunity Nvidia Engagement and its separately verified NVIDIA Engagement Status. After an association becomes inactive, preserve history and stop requiring new engagement input. Status choices and the Opportunity API mapping must be verified before enabling this rule; do not substitute the association Active/Inactive status.
  • CPQ quote inputs: required Quote Purpose (Budgetary / Best and Final); required Timing Urgency defaulting to Standard; Urgent requires Urgency Reason. Competitor Pricing is optional on the quote. Validate through the Quoting activity; these are not additional Opportunity fields.
  • For forward moves through numbered sales stages, enforce all prerequisite stage gates crossed, including assignment/acceptance, discovery, technical fit/POC, Technical Win, procurement and OM booking authority. Apply the same checks to UI, imports, integrations and automation; a stage skip cannot bypass a gate. New sales opportunities start at 0, or at 1 only for the documented assigned seller-self-qualified path. Loss uses its own loss-entry rules and does not require completing earlier sales gates. Preserve the documented Stage 6 → 5 quote-correction path and booked-record locks.
  • Automatically maintain Forecast Category (Sales) = Pipeline on entry and each permitted stage change. Apply through UI, imports and integrations; prevent or correct a value that conflicts with the stage. Keep this separate from standard Salesforce forecast fields.

Automations and validations

  • Restrict the 6 → 7 confirmation to Order Management.
  • OM must confirm order reconciliation before 6 → 7; a nonblank Order Review Status is not sufficient. Configuration prerequisite: map the existing processing, Booked-Complete, booked-with-exception, conditional/demo and revised-PO states to allowed/blocked transitions, with the required exception evidence and authority. RevOps and OM must approve that mapping before enabling the booking gate; do not silently accept every Booked value or invent new picklist choices.
  • Disable quote creation / revision; retain reference access. Keep forecast at Commit until booking.
  • At entry to Won decision (6), require Won Reason and Business Value with specific win-reason detail. Carry forward Technical Value and Operational Value. For amounts ≥ $1M also require Resources Engaged. Capture interview eligibility for New Business, Existing Business and Renewal: Yes requires an interview Contact; No requires WL Reason Cannot Approach. Above $1M notify the Sales Manager and copy the AE; manager submits the reviewed Win wire to Enablement / #win-wire within two business days. Above $2M also require the Deal Team Win Report. Historical closed records are not retro-validated.
  • Review lead time and logistics; retain the Stage 4 installation and Stage 5 shipping/delivery field gates. Reconcile with OM at booking.
  • If OM finds a quote/product/price discrepancy in Stage 6, record the reason and return the opportunity to Stage 5 for Sales/SE correction in CPQ. Revalidate the procurement and Won decision entry gates before returning to 6; do not resend the same win notification. Quote creation/revision stays blocked outside Stages 2–5. After booking, use the existing change-order/amendment process linked to the booked order; do not reopen the booked opportunity or bypass its quote and Close Date locks. Any amendment needing a new sales quote uses a separately linked opportunity in Stages 2–5.
  • Track separate Next Step Reviewed At and SE Next Step Reviewed At system timestamps, reusing existing history where it supports weekly reporting. An AE review action confirms Next Step and expected Close Date at least weekly, even when their text/date is unchanged; it stamps AE reviewer/time without changing the booked date. SE reviews its technical next action separately. Report open Stage 1–6 records not reviewed for seven days; unrelated edits do not reset freshness. Confirm the timestamp storage mapping before implementation.
  • An active Associated accounts record linked to the canonical NVIDIA Account makes NVIDIA relevant. On that association activation and on stage advancement, require the existing Opportunity Nvidia Engagement and its separately verified NVIDIA Engagement Status. After an association becomes inactive, preserve history and stop requiring new engagement input. Status choices and the Opportunity API mapping must be verified before enabling this rule; do not substitute the association Active/Inactive status.
  • Create or link the coordinating PS deployment Case at Won decision (6), using the existing Opportunity PS Case and Order Case(WO) relationships after confirming their targets. GSS starts deployment planning with Sales/SE and OM in Stage 6. Before booking at 7, identify the GSS case owner and document shipping, on-site receiving and installation/deployment plans, dates, responsibilities and dependencies on the Case and linked Order. Avoid duplicate cases on stage re-entry; associate later Orders with the same coordinating case and retain subordinate workstream cases where needed.
  • Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here.
  • For forward moves through numbered sales stages, enforce all prerequisite stage gates crossed, including assignment/acceptance, discovery, technical fit/POC, Technical Win, procurement and OM booking authority. Apply the same checks to UI, imports, integrations and automation; a stage skip cannot bypass a gate. New sales opportunities start at 0, or at 1 only for the documented assigned seller-self-qualified path. Loss uses its own loss-entry rules and does not require completing earlier sales gates. Preserve the documented Stage 6 → 5 quote-correction path and booked-record locks.
  • Automatically maintain Forecast Category (Sales) = Commit on entry and each permitted stage change. Apply through UI, imports and integrations; prevent or correct a value that conflicts with the stage. Keep this separate from standard Salesforce forecast fields.

Automations and validations

  • OM controls booking confirmation and locks Close Date at Stage 7; GSS coordinates the deployment Case.
  • Order Management and GSS coordinate shipping, receiving and deployment through Stages 7–8. Stage 7 is the confirmed/booked PO with locked booked facts and Close Date; Stage 8 Invoice is reached when the complete in-scope order has been delivered on site. Shipment alone does not prove receipt. OM verifies receipt with GSS against every non-cancelled booked item; partial deliveries remain in 7 and approved scope changes are recorded on the Order. Keep promised/scheduled ship dates, actual shipping, receipt and planned/actual deployment distinct on the Order/Case. Record and reconcile invoice evidence on the existing Order and Opportunity references; financial invoice date remains distinct from on-site delivery. Do not create a new Opportunity deployment-status or completion-date field.
  • Create or link the coordinating PS deployment Case at Won decision (6), using the existing Opportunity PS Case and Order Case(WO) relationships after confirming their targets. GSS starts deployment planning with Sales/SE and OM in Stage 6. Before booking at 7, identify the GSS case owner and document shipping, on-site receiving and installation/deployment plans, dates, responsibilities and dependencies on the Case and linked Order. Avoid duplicate cases on stage re-entry; associate later Orders with the same coordinating case and retain subordinate workstream cases where needed.
  • Capture the owner/channel classification at creation. An opportunity created and owned solely by the partner/channel is Partner-only; later ownership transfers do not rewrite that origin. Otherwise, the earliest qualifying partner association or approved deal-registration event before Stage 3 is Partner-Led; the first event in Stages 3–6 is Joint. Evaluate attribution immediately before entry to Closed won (7) and freeze automated credit at booking. Associations added at Stage 7 or later remain visible but do not change booked credit. A management override may correct reporting with an audited reason; blank override uses automated Partner Contribution. Before migration the qualifying association is Primary Partner; after migration it is the first partner in Associated accounts. End Customer-only associations do not trigger partner credit. Retain event date, event stage and origin evidence; stage regression, partner removal and routine ownership changes never reset that evidence. No qualifying event retains Sales only / existing no-partner disposition.
  • Quoting remains disabled; use the agreed amendment/change-order path for booked corrections.
  • Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here.
  • Verified complete on-site delivery controls entry to Stage 8; pending financial invoicing does not block that transition. Invoice Reference and the related Order Invoice Date are required when the invoice is issued, not before delivery-led completion. OM continues invoice reconciliation on the Order after Stage 8; retain all related invoice references and dates. Do not enter placeholder invoice values or use financial Invoice Date as the delivery/time-to-value milestone.
  • For forward moves through numbered sales stages, enforce all prerequisite stage gates crossed, including assignment/acceptance, discovery, technical fit/POC, Technical Win, procurement and OM booking authority. Apply the same checks to UI, imports, integrations and automation; a stage skip cannot bypass a gate. New sales opportunities start at 0, or at 1 only for the documented assigned seller-self-qualified path. Loss uses its own loss-entry rules and does not require completing earlier sales gates. Preserve the documented Stage 6 → 5 quote-correction path and booked-record locks.

GSS manages delivery/setup, the deployment checklist and completion evidence using existing Case fields and workstream relationships; confirm the exact status/date mappings before configuration. Track the sales-defined value plan, target timing, achieved Yes/No, assessment date and supporting context on the Case. Pending outcome stays blank; No requires the gap and agreed disposition. GSS uses results to inform renewals and upsell through separately linked sales opportunities. The original opportunity ends at Stage 8 and is never held open, advanced or reopened to wait for deployment or value realization.

Forecast Category (Sales)Picklist (source inventory; control not verified)

Existing single-select sales forecast picklist; add Pre-pipeline. The stage mapping is defined below. Retain other existing choices and history; confirm stored/API values before configuration.

Proposed inputs
  • Pre-pipeline — new picklist value
Existing sales forecast classification used to report the opportunity’s position in the sales process.Change inputsSystem-maintained · creation and permitted stage changesAutomatically set Forecast Category (Sales) to Loss. Reuse the existing field; add the Pre-pipeline choice. System-maintained, not a separate seller input. Creation / Stage 0 → Pre-pipeline; Stages 1–2 → Pipeline; Stages 3–4 → Upside; Stages 5–6 → Commit; Stages 7–8 → Won; Closed lost → Loss.

Lost deal review

AE and Sales Manager document the decision, loss reason, competitive context, operational / technical / business gaps, lessons and follow-up. Qualifying losses require a Deal Team Loss Report within two business days. Evaluate thresholds using the stage immediately before Closed lost.

Notification recipients for New / Existing Business: AE, Sales Manager, KD, Gary Kortye, Colin, Sales VP, Omer and Guido. Renewals add Kevin Hicks. Include opportunity age, partner context and the loss fields. Resolve recipients to current users or maintained groups during implementation.

CI selects interviews weekly; the AE provides a warm introduction within two business days if requested. Reports and interview summaries remain restricted; never post a loss report to #win-wire. The interview does not delay the report.

Win/Loss rules for this design: ≥$1M requires Resources Engaged; >$1M triggers Win wire; >$2M adds the full Win Report. Capture at entry to Won decision (6), distinct from booking at 7. Include Renewals in interview capture, keep Competitor Lost To loss-only, capture competition in Stage 2 and include Relationship once in Won Reason.

Ongoing activities

  • Lost deal review and follow-up

Automations and validations

  • Require Loss Reason, Loss Reason Details, Competitor Lost To and Lost — Next Step Recommendation. Allow Not lost to competitor; require Competitor Detail (if Other) when either competitor field uses Other. Opportunity Misqualified is allowed only when the prior stage was 0. Capture interview eligibility and its conditional contact/reason. Notify on loss from prior Stage ≥3 with amount >$1M, or prior Stage ≥2 with amount >$5M; retain prior stage for evaluation. Set forecast to Loss and disable quoting. Historical closed records are not retro-validated; an unrealized post-sales outcome does not turn a booked sale into a loss.
  • A qualifying Deal Team Loss Report is due within two business days; keep it restricted and never post it to #win-wire.
  • At Closed lost, the AE confirms the existing Close Date reflects the actual loss decision date. Retain date-change history. Weekly open-pipeline review requirements stop on loss; do not change previously closed records retroactively or replace a booked Close Date with a post-sales outcome date.
  • Automatically maintain Forecast Category (Sales) = Loss on entry and each permitted stage change. Apply through UI, imports and integrations; prevent or correct a value that conflicts with the stage. Keep this separate from standard Salesforce forecast fields.

Stages 2–5 · Sales + Sales Engineering

Quoting

Process

Use CPQ to add products and develop the quote. The selected Primary Quote drives Opportunity Amount and adds/synchronizes Opportunity Products. Select it by Stage 4; revise quotes only within Stages 2–5.

New quote fields

Add these fields to the CPQ quote creation and editing forms. Quote Purpose is separate from the existing CPQ Type field.

Field / typeInputsRequired / defaultPurpose / help text
Quote PurposePicklistNew fieldBudgetary / Best and FinalRequired; no default.Identify whether the quote is budgetary or best and final.
Timing UrgencyPicklistNew fieldStandard / UrgentRequired; default Standard on new quotes.Flag urgency only when needed; selecting Urgent makes Urgency Reason required.
Urgency ReasonText areaNew fieldFree text; no fixed dropdown choices.Required only when Timing Urgency = Urgent.Explain why the quote is urgent. Whitespace-only input does not satisfy the requirement. Proposed text limit to be set during configuration.
Competitor PricingText areaNew fieldFree text; no fixed dropdown choices.Optional.Help text: Do we have visibility into competitor pricing? Capture available pricing context; leave blank when unknown. Proposed text limit to be set during configuration.

Quote validations

On CPQ quote creation and editable quote saves, require Quote Purpose = Budgetary or Best and Final and Timing Urgency = Standard or Urgent. Default Timing Urgency to Standard on new quotes; Quote Purpose has no default and must be selected. If Timing Urgency = Urgent, show and require a nonblank Urgency Reason; Standard does not require a reason. Competitor Pricing is optional. Apply the validation across supported UI and integration save paths; keep quote creation/revision restricted to Stages 2–5.

Retain existing Primary Quote synchronization to Opportunity Amount and Products. These additions belong on the Quote, not the Opportunity. Historical locked quotes do not require a bulk backfill; apply the rules when creating or editing an editable quote.

Creating a CPQ quote

The supplied New Quote screenshot shows Account and Opportunity context, Name, Legal Entity, Bill To Account, Ship To Account, Bill To Contact, Ship To Contact, Primary Contact and Expires On. Legal Entity is the only visible required marker in that creation dialog. Name has a 128-character limit in the inspected edit form. The full edit form additionally marks DDN Terms & Conditions, Bill To Account, Ship To Account and Install Countries required; validate those downstream requirements separately from the create dialog.

Proposed creation gate: linked Opportunity must be in Stages 2–5. Populate the quote by adding products, then use the Primary Quote to update Opportunity Amount and Products. No quote was created or saved during this review.

Quote-line columns: Line Name, Product, Config Part, List Unit Price, Customer Unit Price, Total Discount (%), Quantity, Net Total, Covered Asset and Product Status. Quote-line inputs were not edited; keep product/pricing detail on CPQ lines.

Related tabs inspected: Lines & Groups (product / quote-line information, groups, pricing guidance and additional documents); Sales Orders; Files & Attachments (quote documents, notes and attachments); Approvals & History (approvals, archived approvals, field history and activities). These remain related records, not new Opportunity fields.

Operating rules

Create-form requirements and the captured quote inventory are below. Quote calculation/product synchronization needs regression validation; do not replace it with manual Opportunity amount or product entry. If OM finds a quote/product/price discrepancy in Stage 6, record the reason and return the opportunity to Stage 5 for Sales/SE correction in CPQ. Revalidate the procurement and Won decision entry gates before returning to 6; do not resend the same win notification. Quote creation/revision stays blocked outside Stages 2–5. After booking, use the existing change-order/amendment process linked to the booked order; do not reopen the booked opportunity or bypass its quote and Close Date locks. Any amendment needing a new sales quote uses a separately linked opportunity in Stages 2–5. On CPQ quote creation and editable quote saves, require Quote Purpose = Budgetary or Best and Final and Timing Urgency = Standard or Urgent. Default Timing Urgency to Standard on new quotes; Quote Purpose has no default and must be selected. If Timing Urgency = Urgent, show and require a nonblank Urgency Reason; Standard does not require a reason. Competitor Pricing is optional. Apply the validation across supported UI and integration save paths; keep quote creation/revision restricted to Stages 2–5.

All ongoing activities ↑

Stages Creation–1 · Marketing + Sales / BDR + Partner teams + RevOps

Conversion tracking

Process

MAP supplies MQL (Marketing Qualified Lead) to the existing Contact lifecycle. PAX.AI supplies AQL (Agentic Qualified Lead) to the Contact. These are distinct entry paths, not mandatory sequential stages. Creating an opportunity records SAL (Sales Accepted Lead) for its linked conversion Contact and the new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle. Inbound marketing, BDR, partner/deal-registration and other non-self-qualified intake enters Stage 0 for SAL review. Entry to Stage 1 records SQL (Sales Qualified Lead) after acceptance and the source-dependent CHAMPS gate. A seller creating a self-qualified opportunity directly in Stage 1 may auto-accept and qualify it: record SAL and SQL events at creation, with the seller and direct-entry path; do not manufacture prior MQL/AQL events. Sales-generated CHAMPS remains required before Stage 2.

Connected fields

Field / typeInputsCurrent purposeChangeUpdated purpose / placement
Conversion ContactRelated records

Select/link an existing Salesforce Contact; no new buying-role choice is required. Exact originating-contact relationship mapping must be confirmed.

Associates existing Contacts with the opportunity and records their buying roles.Extend trackingCapture a resolved originating Contact at creation for conversion tracking; maintain buying roles through discovery and require at least one assigned Opportunity Contact Role before Stage 2. These are separate checks. Reuse a verified existing originating/primary Contact relationship or a designated Contact Role; confirm the exact relationship and creation transaction before configuration. Primary Contact remains optional as a committee designation unless it is the approved conversion-link mapping. Do not require every buying-committee member at creation. Use an explicitly designated originating/conversion Contact linked through Opportunity Contact Roles; reuse a supported existing primary/originating Contact relationship before adding storage. Require a resolved Contact association for creation/conversion tracking and before Stage 1; do not update every buying-committee member or infer the person from an account alone. The account is the linked Contact account in the same deal context; flag conflicting associations for review. Extend the existing Contact lifecycle and Account Lifecycle Stage (ALS) mappings rather than creating parallel Contact/Account lifecycle fields. Show per-contact conversion and related-opportunity evidence on the Account; preserve its current lifecycle semantics and customer states until the rollup mapping is approved. One new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle field shows SAL/SQL for this opportunity; retain it as the last conversion milestone after discovery, separate from sales Stage.

A rejected SAL does not become SQL or automatically become a Closed lost opportunity; retain its rejection disposition for conversion reporting.

Lifecycle events

MAP → MQL or PAX.AI → AQL on Contact → opportunity created: SAL → Stage 1: SQL.

Seller-qualified direct entry records SAL + SQL together; it does not invent an MQL/AQL event. SAL lifecycle entry and the reviewer’s acceptance decision are separate events.

Current field evidence

SAL AE, SAL Type, SAL Status, SAL Submission Date, SAL Start Date and SAL Completion Date were confirmed in Salesforce on 17 Sep 2026. They were read-only on the inspected form. Acceptance/rejection dates and notes were not exposed as separate controls; their existing mappings and limits remain to be verified.

Future MAP dependency

Future dependency — Marketing MAP program: today Sourcing Campaign remains the existing Pardot campaign lookup (current system described by Marketing). At the MAP cutover it will become Marketing Source, mapped from the marketing automation platform source. This is a future field/integration change, not a rename or replacement in the current release. Confirm source IDs, data model, historical campaign crosswalk and multi-touch reporting with Marketing before migration; preserve current attribution and historical campaign references. Opportunity Source still provides the high-level source classification.

Operating rules

Creating the opportunity records the SAL lifecycle milestone, not the AE acceptance decision. Use existing SAL Status for Pending review, Accepted and Rejected business meanings, mapped to its actual current choices before configuration. SAL AE identifies the accountable seller. For inbound records, prevent entry to Stage 1 while pending or rejected; acceptance alone does not bypass CHAMPS or assignment. For Marketing-source SALs, require an acceptance date when accepted; require rejection date and explanatory notes when rejected. Notes remain available throughout review. Stamp actor and decision time through the acceptance/rejection action rather than manual date entry. Retain submissions, start and completion history separately: do not equate SAL Start Date with acceptance or SAL Completion Date with rejection without verifying their definitions. A rejected SAL does not become SQL or automatically become a Closed lost opportunity; retain its rejection disposition for conversion reporting. Use an explicitly designated originating/conversion Contact linked through Opportunity Contact Roles; reuse a supported existing primary/originating Contact relationship before adding storage. Require a resolved Contact association for creation/conversion tracking and before Stage 1; do not update every buying-committee member or infer the person from an account alone. The account is the linked Contact account in the same deal context; flag conflicting associations for review. Extend the existing Contact lifecycle and Account Lifecycle Stage (ALS) mappings rather than creating parallel Contact/Account lifecycle fields. Show per-contact conversion and related-opportunity evidence on the Account; preserve its current lifecycle semantics and customer states until the rollup mapping is approved. One new Opportunity Conversion Lifecycle field shows SAL/SQL for this opportunity; retain it as the last conversion milestone after discovery, separate from sales Stage. Retain source, qualification channel (MAP / PAX.AI / seller / partner / BDR), event timestamp, actor/system, Contact, Account and Opportunity relationship in conversion history. Reuse suitable existing lifecycle history/event storage, extending only missing data. Record MQL or AQL at Contact qualification; SAL at opportunity creation; accepted/rejected decision separately; SQL on first Stage 1 entry. Preserve every episode for multiple opportunities or requalification and deduplicate retries with the source event/episode ID. Late MQL/AQL messages, stage regression and a new Stage 0 opportunity must not downgrade a Contact or Account already further through its lifecycle. Keep opportunity-level events so separate deals remain reportable. Report MQL→SAL→SQL and AQL→SAL→SQL as separate cohorts, including rejected/pending SALs and elapsed time. Seller-direct SQLs remain a separate cohort; never inflate inbound conversion by fabricating MQL/AQL history. PAX.AI is the qualification channel, not a newly approved Opportunity Source choice. Its integration must provide the underlying source and applicable sourcing lookup under the current source-attribution rules; resolve that mapping before automated opportunity creation. Do not invent a campaign, sourcing user or partner, and do not classify an AI-origin record as seller-self-qualified simply because an integration created it. Record the assigned AE acceptance decision with actor and time separately from SAL creation and SQL qualification. RevOps acting for the AE must retain evidence of that acceptance. The later 0 → 1 handoff checks acceptance, assignment and CHAMPS before recording SQL. All non-self-qualified inbound intake—including Marketing, partner/deal registration, BDR-origin and PAX.AI—must complete all five existing CHAMPS fields before Stage 1. Determine this from preserved intake origin and qualification path; reassignment to an AE does not remove the gate. Seller-self-qualified opportunities may start in Stage 1 and must complete CHAMPS before Stage 2. Keep CHAMPS - Challenge separate from Identify Pain. Capture a resolved originating Contact at creation for conversion tracking; maintain buying roles through discovery and require at least one assigned Opportunity Contact Role before Stage 2. These are separate checks. Reuse a verified existing originating/primary Contact relationship or a designated Contact Role; confirm the exact relationship and creation transaction before configuration. Primary Contact remains optional as a committee designation unless it is the approved conversion-link mapping. Do not require every buying-committee member at creation.

All ongoing activities ↑

Operating rules

Require one primary Role On Deal and one Account Classification per Associated Account record. Keep the existing single-select role and extend its choices to Transacting, Influencer, Solution / Technical Advisor, Implementation / Services, End Customer and Other. Otherwise, the earliest qualifying partner association or approved deal-registration event before Stage 3 is Partner-Led; the first event in Stages 3–6 is Joint. Evaluate attribution immediately before entry to Closed won (7) and freeze automated credit at booking. Associations added at Stage 7 or later remain visible but do not change booked credit. A management override may correct reporting with an audited reason; blank override uses automated Partner Contribution. Before migration the qualifying association is Primary Partner; after migration it is the first partner in Associated accounts. End Customer-only associations do not trigger partner credit. Retain event date, event stage and origin evidence; stage regression, partner removal and routine ownership changes never reset that evidence. No qualifying event retains Sales only / existing no-partner disposition.

Capture a resolved originating Contact at creation for conversion tracking; maintain buying roles through discovery and require at least one assigned Opportunity Contact Role before Stage 2. These are separate checks. Reuse a verified existing originating/primary Contact relationship or a designated Contact Role; confirm the exact relationship and creation transaction before configuration. Primary Contact remains optional as a committee designation unless it is the approved conversion-link mapping. Do not require every buying-committee member at creation.

All ongoing activities ↑

WL Reason Cannot ApproachText · proposed; limit to define
Proposed inputs

Proposed field; no current input control. Use the proposed type shown above.

No existing field confirmed; proposed capture.New fieldShow and require only when OK to Interview = No. Hide or disable otherwise and never validate this hidden input. Reuse the corresponding Win/Loss rollout field if already deployed; confirm mapping before creating a field.

Win wire content and distribution

Strategic outcome; the win; business impact; technical and operational impact; why DDN won; strategic implications; team recognition; final takeaway. Use the customer, amount and strategic outcome in the subject.

Deal Team Reports and interview summaries remain restricted. The reviewed Win wire is for internal GTM; external reuse requires separate approval. CI selects interviews weekly; the AE provides an introduction within two business days when requested. Interviews do not delay reports.

Win/Loss rules for this design: ≥$1M requires Resources Engaged; >$1M triggers Win wire; >$2M adds the full Win Report. Capture at entry to Won decision (6), distinct from booking at 7. Include Renewals in interview capture, keep Competitor Lost To loss-only, capture competition in Stage 2 and include Relationship once in Won Reason.

Operating rules

At entry to Won decision (6), require Won Reason and Business Value with specific win-reason detail. Carry forward Technical Value and Operational Value. For amounts ≥ $1M also require Resources Engaged. Capture interview eligibility for New Business, Existing Business and Renewal: Yes requires an interview Contact; No requires WL Reason Cannot Approach. Above $1M notify the Sales Manager and copy the AE; manager submits the reviewed Win wire to Enablement / #win-wire within two business days. Above $2M also require the Deal Team Win Report. Historical closed records are not retro-validated.

All ongoing activities ↑

Operating rules

Order Management and GSS coordinate shipping, receiving and deployment through Stages 7–8. Stage 7 is the confirmed/booked PO with locked booked facts and Close Date; Stage 8 Invoice is reached when the complete in-scope order has been delivered on site. Shipment alone does not prove receipt. OM verifies receipt with GSS against every non-cancelled booked item; partial deliveries remain in 7 and approved scope changes are recorded on the Order. Keep promised/scheduled ship dates, actual shipping, receipt and planned/actual deployment distinct on the Order/Case. Record and reconcile invoice evidence on the existing Order and Opportunity references; financial invoice date remains distinct from on-site delivery. Do not create a new Opportunity deployment-status or completion-date field. Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here. Verified complete on-site delivery controls entry to Stage 8; pending financial invoicing does not block that transition. Invoice Reference and the related Order Invoice Date are required when the invoice is issued, not before delivery-led completion. OM continues invoice reconciliation on the Order after Stage 8; retain all related invoice references and dates. Do not enter placeholder invoice values or use financial Invoice Date as the delivery/time-to-value milestone. OM must confirm order reconciliation before 6 → 7; a nonblank Order Review Status is not sufficient. Configuration prerequisite: map the existing processing, Booked-Complete, booked-with-exception, conditional/demo and revised-PO states to allowed/blocked transitions, with the required exception evidence and authority. RevOps and OM must approve that mapping before enabling the booking gate; do not silently accept every Booked value or invent new picklist choices.

All ongoing activities ↑

Stages 6–8 · GSS + Order Management

Deployment case

Process

Create or link the coordinating PS deployment Case at Won decision (6), using the existing Opportunity PS Case and Order Case(WO) relationships after confirming their targets. GSS starts deployment planning with Sales/SE and OM in Stage 6. Before booking at 7, identify the GSS case owner and document shipping, on-site receiving and installation/deployment plans, dates, responsibilities and dependencies on the Case and linked Order. Avoid duplicate cases on stage re-entry; associate later Orders with the same coordinating case and retain subordinate workstream cases where needed. Deliver the Deal continues on the PS Case, not as numbered Opportunity stages. GSS manages delivery/setup, the deployment checklist and completion evidence using existing Case fields and workstream relationships; confirm the exact status/date mappings before configuration. The coordinating Case covers all in-scope workstreams; closing one case alone does not prove full deployment. Track the sales-defined value plan, target timing, achieved Yes/No, assessment date and supporting context on the Case. Pending outcome stays blank; No requires the gap and agreed disposition. GSS uses results to inform renewals and upsell through separately linked sales opportunities. The original opportunity ends at Stage 8 and is never held open, advanced or reopened to wait for deployment or value realization.

Connected fields

Case automations and validations

Store Value Tracking Start Date on the coordinating PS Case. Preserve the first Stage 8 entry / verified on-site-delivery milestone from the linked Opportunity once; do not reset it on re-entry, retries or case reassignment. This retains the agreed delivery-based time-to-value baseline, not the financial Invoice Date or the Case creation date at Stage 6. On the Case, calculate Time to Value only when Value Achieved? = Yes and Value Outcome Confirmed Date is on/after that start; pending/No stays blank. A missing start is an integration exception, not a reason to reopen or hold the Opportunity.

Require a plan and target date for value tracking; require outcome, assessment date and context only when GSS completes the value assessment. Do not equate a deployment Case closure with value achievement. Preserve all workstream evidence and the original booked Opportunity.

Operating rules

Order Management and GSS coordinate shipping, receiving and deployment through Stages 7–8. Stage 7 is the confirmed/booked PO with locked booked facts and Close Date; Stage 8 Invoice is reached when the complete in-scope order has been delivered on site. Shipment alone does not prove receipt. OM verifies receipt with GSS against every non-cancelled booked item; partial deliveries remain in 7 and approved scope changes are recorded on the Order. Keep promised/scheduled ship dates, actual shipping, receipt and planned/actual deployment distinct on the Order/Case. Record and reconcile invoice evidence on the existing Order and Opportunity references; financial invoice date remains distinct from on-site delivery. Do not create a new Opportunity deployment-status or completion-date field. Store Value Tracking Start Date on the coordinating PS Case. Preserve the first Stage 8 entry / verified on-site-delivery milestone from the linked Opportunity once; do not reset it on re-entry, retries or case reassignment. This retains the agreed delivery-based time-to-value baseline, not the financial Invoice Date or the Case creation date at Stage 6. On the Case, calculate Time to Value only when Value Achieved? = Yes and Value Outcome Confirmed Date is on/after that start; pending/No stays blank. A missing start is an integration exception, not a reason to reopen or hold the Opportunity. Applicability to confirm — OM and GSS must define the logistics dates, deployment-Case requirement and Stage 8 completion evidence for renewals, software/services, Internal Transfer, Non-Revenue and No Shipping required orders. The physical-delivery path applies where shipping/deployment is in scope. Do not invent delivery dates, create a dummy Case or treat shipment as receipt to satisfy it. Confirm the applicable alternative before enabling universal logistics/Case validation; no new exception status or field is approved here.

All ongoing activities ↑

Outside the one-page summary

Optional opp fields

Additional customer, competitor, solution, operations and system context. Review the Inputs column for dropdown choices and character limits. These fields do not introduce new stage-exit requirements; existing platform requirements and system ownership remain in force.

Existing fulfillment and service fields are retained on their respective objects: see the Order inventory and PS Case inventory. They do not become additional Opportunity fields or stage requirements.
